# Session 27 â€” Gayle's Warning and the Dandruff Lab

*2 November 2026*

The Defracturers had barely settled back into life at their [[02 Places/Rakara/Somnolent/The Defracturers' Bastion|Bastion]] when a wounded messenger, an increasingly exasperated headman and an ill-advised business arrangement sent them racing south of [[02 Places/Rakara/Somnolent/Somnolent|Somnolent]]. By the end of the day, they had discovered an uncomfortable truth about the [[04 Organisations/Broken Fang|Broken Fang]]'s drug operation, survived an unnatural creature on the road and found themselves considering whether the best way to destroy a Dragon's Dandruff laboratory was to recruit its employees.

## A Warning at the Door

[[03 People/Bastion Staff/Joe-Seff Gorbels|Joe-Seff Gorbels]] was preparing to run an errand for Ballar when [[03 People/Notable Figures/Gayle Bramble|Gayle Bramble]], a deputy of Somnolent's watch, collapsed on the Bastion's doorstep. Her clothes were torn and rain-soaked, and she was bruised, cut and desperately exhausted. [[03 People/Bastion Staff/Mariasz Salhum|Mariasz]] hurried to help, with the rest of the household contributing varying degrees of assistance. Once Mariasz had healed her, Gayle was taken upstairs to recover, despite [[03 People/Bastion Staff/Derrick Lowgins|Derrick]]'s determined argument that Ballar's bedroom was the most efficient destination.

Gayle brought terrible news. She and [[03 People/Notable Figures/Job Ashmere|Job Ashmere]] had traced [[03 People/Notable Figures/Eden Carrion|Eden Carrion]] and [[03 People/Notable Figures/Summer Quaresh|Summer Quaresh]] to **[[02 Places/Rakara/Stone Pass|Stone Pass]]**, between Erzhin and Khaali territory. When Job called for the pair to stop, Eden and Summer appeared to turn deathly pale. Suddenly, the watch found itself surrounded by an extraordinary gathering of undead: humans, elves, orcs, dwarves and even ogres, apparently being directed by a figure in a dark coat.

The watch was overwhelmed. Job and the others held the creatures back long enough for Gayle to escape. Her horse eventually died from the strain, and she spent much of the following week making her way back without adequate food or water. She had been trying to reach [[03 People/Notable Figures/Romayne Drayton|Romayne Drayton]] when she collapsed at the Bastion instead. **Job's fate, along with that of the other watch members, remains unknown.** Nor could Gayle establish from what she saw whether Eden and Summer were responsible for the undead or were themselves frightened by them. The pass was thought to be roughly three days' ride away if the party travelled directly there.

## The Headman's Patience

Romayne arrived with [[03 People/Notable Figures/Alaaric Hammerfall|Alaaric Hammerfall]] and a substantial dossier of grievances. While the Defracturers had been away, the search for Eden and Summer had depleted Somnolent's already stretched watch. [[03 People/Notable Figures/Antoinne Cassan|Antoinne Cassan]] was missing, the [[02 Places/Rakara/Somnolent/One Ball Inn|One Ball Inn]] had burned, and the [[03 People/Notable Figures/Ervain Megbanas|Old Grey Pike]] had slipped out of town. Romayne believed that much of the Bastion's staff could have been more useful during the crisis.

Mariasz objected, and Ballar immediately came to her defence. In his view, the staff had been hired to maintain the Bastion, not police Somnolent on its behalf. Alaaric was ready with the relevant clause of their contract, however, which allowed the town to requisition staff for duties relating to its safety. When Ghorgadush's thriving private pie business was raised as evidence that there had been time for other work, Ballar finally administered his chosen punishment: **Mariasz was suspended for the rest of the day, on full pay**.

That did not improve matters. Romayne also wished to discuss Ballar having thrown [[03 People/Notable Figures/Terrick Lowgins|Terrick Lowgins]] through a neighbour's window after learning of his treatment of Derrick. Ballar maintained that he had been defending a victim of abuse. Romayne pointed out that this did not absolve him of responsibility for the assault or the broken window, and that the alleged abuse could be investigated properly. Ballar, now convinced that Somnolent was punishing him for defending the downtrodden, stormed out to surrender himself to the authorities.

With the room temporarily quieter, Romayne explained his immediate priorities. He wanted dangerous Ardynian relics reviewed and, if necessary, secured in Alaaric's safe, with arrangements for the party to access them should they actually be needed. He also wanted help recovering Job and the missing watch, but the immediate task was to investigate the **[[02 Places/Rakara/Dragon's Dandruff Laboratory|Dragon's Dandruff operation south of Somnolent]]**. The relics remained in Ballar's possession, and the party had not yet agreed to hand them over. Meanwhile, Alaaric confirmed that he had told Mariasz about the Pike's departure before the One Ball fire; whatever had happened to that information, Erelmor was furious that his quarry had disappeared.

## A Hero Behind Bars

Ballar reached the Shackles, surrendered his equipment and installed himself in a cell. [[03 People/Mentioned Figures/Maxine Hintermeier|Maxine Hintermeier]] was understandably confused: Ballar had not actually been arrested, and domestic abuse was very much illegal in Somnolent, despite his insistence otherwise. Ballar was unmoved by both legal clarification and the offer to leave.

Eventually his companions arrived and persuaded him to come away, with n'Dong announcing that his bail had been paid and his sentence suspended. Before leaving, Ballar also paid **25 gold** to secure the release of a fellow prisoner known as the *chicken man*. The latter set off in the direction of the nearest farm, a development which did little to reassure Ieuan about Ballar's judgement of character.

Before setting out, Ballar returned to the Bastion and gave Derrick two further jobs. The deceased Broken Fang scout was to be buried, while [[03 People/Notable Figures/Mireya Dinak|Mireya]]'s preserved body and a diamond were to be taken to [[03 People/Notable Figures/Elmwood Bluevine|Elmwood Bluevine]] for resurrection. Derrick appeared to understand the distinction, although his first move was to drag the burned body into Gayle's room. **Neither the burial nor Mireya's revival was confirmed before the party departed.**

## The Least Reliable Advance Party

Unbeknownst to his companions, n'Dong had already hired [[03 People/Mentioned Figures/Yama and Spopovich|Yama and Spopovich]], the two drug-fuelled pit fighters responsible for the previous day's market brawl. He supplied them with yet more [[06 Library/Campaign Reference/Dragon's Dandruff|Dragon's Dandruff]], a rough copy of the route to the laboratory and instructions to scout it, steal what they could and disrupt the operation if the opportunity presented itself. They eagerly set off at a sprint.

The problem became apparent when the rest of the party learned of this arrangement. Rather than allow two unpredictable, heavily intoxicated men to arrive first, the Defracturers rode after them. Ieuan sent his owl familiar, Noctua, ahead to delay them, only for the bird to receive a punch for its trouble. After catching up, the group eventually convinced the pair to abandon the expedition and return towards Somnolent, with another supply of drugs and a proposed meeting near the Bastion that night.

As they continued south, Ieuan argued for better communication before anyone made arrangements on the party's behalf. n'Dong explained that he had been attempting to turn the fighters' talents against [[03 People/Notable Figures/Prodosius|Prodosius]] and the Broken Fang, although even he acknowledged that paying them in an addictive stimulant might not have been his finest decision.

## Something in the River

Their journey was interrupted at a river crossing by a swollen, unnatural **slaad-like creature**. Ieuan recognised the danger posed by such aberrations, and the thing bore an unsettling resemblance to the creatures seen beneath the ruined One Ball Inn, although it did not quite match the usual descriptions. The party had little time to examine it before combat began.

n'Dong attempted to take Nail across a fallen log, judged the crossing safe and promptly sent both horse and rider crashing into the river when it gave way. The creature proved capable of a devastating leaping attack, and the party struggled to keep their distance. Ballar held the front line, Enrak and n'Dong attacked with spells, and Ieuan unleashed a pack of spectral wolves while taking on his Starry Form. Erelmor, still nursing his wounded pride from the market fight, landed the finishing blows with his psychic blades and celebrated enthusiastically over the fallen creature. Even Vesper appeared suitably impressed.

After a short rest, the party continued towards the farm marked on n'Dong's map.

## An Old Friend, an Invisible Rogue and a Very Visible Rat

The laboratory occupied a working barn, filled with alchemical vessels, volatile substances and the obvious signs of large-scale drug production. Before entering, n'Dong shared what he had heard of its supplier: a three-fingered Khaali woman named [[03 People/Notable Figures/Karessa Val|Karessa]]. The name stirred an unpleasant memory for Enrak.

**Karessa Val** had once been his friend and rival among the Khaali Fire Mages. Years earlier, Enrak had pushed a magical experiment beyond what Karessa believed was safe, using material from a spellbook he had taken from the Minir archives. The apparatus exploded when Karessa intervened to stop the reaction, costing her two fingers while Enrak escaped largely unharmed. Enrak continued to regard the accident as principally her mistake; Karessa, as the party would soon discover, remembered events rather differently.

Rather than begin with fireballs, the group agreed to scout. Erelmor made himself invisible and did the same for Ieuan, who also transformed into a rat. Inside, they found three quarrelling alchemists: Karessa, Davos and Pella. Ieuan reached the office and discovered the **Dragon's Dandruff recipe**, material concerning production quotas and a threatening message indicating that the workers were under considerable pressure from their employers. He decided that the recipe should not survive. His first approach was to eat it, which went badly for his tiny stomach; shredding the remainder with his teeth proved more practical. Erelmor, meanwhile, collected **six bloodstones worth 50 gold each**.

Having obtained their information, the two scouts attempted to find each other without using the private communication available to them. Their whispered efforts attracted attention. A suspicious alchemist threw flour in their direction, exposing Erelmor's position and turning the stealth mission into an armed confrontation. Ieuan's sudden reappearance as a talking rat did little to clarify matters.

## The Price of Walking Away

The standoff was not immediately fatal. Erelmor threatened to bring the volatile laboratory down around everyone, while Ieuan tried to explain that the visitors meant to free its workers from the Broken Fang. Outside, n'Dong used *Message* to warn Karessa that Enrak was nearby and capable of burning the entire place down. The parties eventually paused long enough to talk.

Karessa's anger at Enrak was still raw. She blamed his recklessness for the accident and explained that she had subsequently been expelled from the Fire Mages. More urgently, she said that **Rhul Slowdrift and Prodosius had threatened the alchemists' families**, with agents capable of reaching them in Khaali territory. Walking away, she believed, would lead to reprisals. Davos seemed willing to leave, but Pella was making good money and had little desire to abandon the enterprise.

n'Dong suggested making the workers' deaths appear to be part of the destruction of the laboratory. The party offered protection, though precisely what that protection would involve remained hazy. Enrak even floated the possibility of allowing them to operate from the Bastion's unused basement, an idea Ballar regarded as unlikely to improve relations with Romayne. The laboratory reportedly held roughly two-thirds of its next shipment, with an estimated **street value of 5,000â€“6,000 gold**; none of this had yet been secured or removed.

Eventually Karessa and Pella agreed to cooperate in luring the guards inside, with the understanding that the party would deal with them and the lab would be destroyed. But the details of the alchemists' escape, their families' safety and any future arrangement at the Bastion remained unsettled.

## An Exercise in Teamwork

Four armed veterans entered the barn expecting a routine problem. Instead, they found the Defracturers, a pack of spectral wolves and a strategy that immediately began to unravel. Erelmor's *Faerie Fire* failed to catch its intended target, leaving Ballar convinced that stepping into the spell's area would get him killed. He spent a considerable part of the opening exchange furiously berating Erelmor and repositioning himself, while the alchemists watched their supposed rescuers argue.

Fortunately, the actual fighting proved more effective. Ieuan's spectral wolves tore through the guards, with his radiant attacks adding to the damage. Enrak and n'Dong hurled *Chromatic Orbs* across the barn, and Ballar finally turned his sword on the enemy rather than his companion. Pella and Karessa also joined in, using dangerous alchemical flasks despite the considerable amount of explosive material still surrounding them.

The veterans hit hard. **n'Dong was knocked unconscious** by a series of attacks, and the others came under heavy crossbow fire. Pella brought down one guard with a volatile detonation. Erelmor then emerged from cover, scored a spectacular critical hit with his psychic blades and brought the last enemy down, describing himself leaping onto the man's back and waving triumphantly at Ballar before finishing him.

The battle was won, although Karessa was less impressed by the group's interpersonal skills than by their fighting. She observed that the Defracturers seemed to have considerable unresolved tension, something Ballar was willing to discuss at length. The session ended there, **before the laboratory was burned, the alchemists were evacuated or n'Dong's condition was resolved**.

## Where Things Stand

- **Gayle's warning:** Gayle survived and is recovering at the Bastion. Job Ashmere and the other watch members remain missing following an undead ambush in Stone Pass. Eden and Summer were sighted there, but their precise involvement is unknown.
- **The laboratory:** Its four guards have been defeated, but the lab and remaining shipment have not yet been destroyed. Karessa, Pella and Davos are still at the scene; their next steps and the proposed cover story remain unresolved.
- **The Broken Fang:** Karessa says Rhul and Prodosius are using threats against the alchemists' families to secure production. The details of the lab's quota and threatening correspondence were discovered, but their wider implications remain to be investigated.
- **n'Dong:** He was left unconscious at the close of the fight. Yama and Spopovich have been sent back towards Somnolent with more Dragon's Dandruff and instructions to meet later.
- **The Bastion:** Romayne expects closer cooperation and wants the Ardynian relics secured. The party has not handed them over. Derrick has been tasked with arranging Mireya's resurrection and burying the scout, but neither outcome is confirmed.
- **The Old Grey Pike:** He has left Somnolent, and the authorities do not know where he went. Erelmor remains determined to find him.

## Character Histories

The opening character exercise also established several glimpses of the Defracturers' younger lives. Ieuan once vanished into the woods for weeks, entirely unaware that his family had organised a desperate search. Ballar's great childhood disgrace was being caught reading fiction in a servants' library and receiving a stern look from his father. n'Dong was punished by the tribe holding him after stealing Dragon's Dandruff, leaving his feet burned on hot coals. Enrak nearly found himself expelled from House Khaali for increasingly scandalous claims about his relationship with Ashka. Erelmor recalled being punished by his father for refusing to lie to his mother about a visit to the tavern.

# Bands of Distortion

The **Bands of Distortion** are an Ardynian relic recovered from the stone golem guarding [[02 Places/Rakara/Rana Viam|Rana Viam]].

The bands were linked to the golem through a **heart stone**. Ieuan determined that removing the stone alongside the relic would prevent the bands from returning to the ruin in the manner of the [[Staff of Broken Time]]. The party stored the bands in a fitted holding case and did not immediately attempt to wear them.

During the parley at Ulsar Quinn's camp, Ulsar revealed that he knew not only about the Bands but also about the heart stone. He claimed he only needed one or the other: if someone failed to attune to the Bands, they would return to the heart stone. Possessing either object would therefore be enough to threaten the party's control of the relic.

## Identified powers and limits

In Session 25, Elmwood examined the Bands and described three abilities:

- **Defensive distortion:** briefly shifting the wearer's place among possible moments to evade or lessen danger.
- **Offensive distortion:** creating a damaging area of disrupted time.
- **Altered outcome:** manipulating an attack so its outcome becomes a critical success or critical failure.

Elmwood reported **five charges** and that some charges return after a long rest. The precise charge cost of each effect, the recharge amount, and the full game statistics have not been established in the chronicle. Attunement remains untested and potentially dangerous.

The full Ardynian vision also depicts bracers worn by Elgira; the vision alone does not establish their identity with the recovered Bands. The party retains both the Bands and their heart stone; no successful attunement is recorded.

## Campaign record

- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 25 - Ashes, Arrogance, and the Blood of Ages|Session 25]] â€” Elmwood explains the properties and warns that attunement requires care.

- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 18 - Frogger, Flames and the Golem's Toll|Session 18 - Frogger, Flames and the Golem's Toll]] - the first failed attempt to defeat the guardian.
- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 19 - Bands, Broken Stone and the Glass Fang|Session 19 - Bands, Broken Stone and the Glass Fang]] - the golem is destroyed and the bands are secured.
- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 21 - Contracts, Command, and a Burning Camp|Session 21 - Contracts, Command, and a Burning Camp]] - Ulsar reveals that the heart stone is a strategic vulnerability as well as a safeguard.

## Session 26 â€” custody and anchor hypothesis

After discussing the relic with Elmwood, the Defracturers elected to **retain both Bands and heart stone** rather than place them in Aalaricâ€™s secure storage or leave them with Bastion staff. No bearer or attunement was selected.

The Bandsâ€™ connection to their heart stone prompted a **hypothesis** that other major Sundering relics may be tied to similar keystones or anchors. This does **not** establish that every relic has one, nor does it prove that Elgiraâ€™s remembered bracers were the Bands. See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 26 - Sundering Relics and the Market Brawl|Session 26]].

## Session 27 â€” custody discussed

Romayne requested review and possible secure custody of dangerous Ardynian artefacts in Alaaricâ€™s safe, with arrangements for access when needed. The Bands and other relics remained with the party; no handover or attunement occurred in the recorded session.

See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

# Corona of the Ashen Choir

A **Corona of the Ashen Choir** is an Ardynian relic associated with the Sundering. During the assault on the Perfidian presence in Somnolent, a fanatic wore a glowing corona and escaped through a portal with one surviving zealot.

In the shared memory activated in Session 25, the Defracturers saw five golden **Crowns of Infinity**, originally built to draw magical energy from those held in their thrall and use it to fuel Ardynia's immortality. The **Corona of the Ashen Choir encountered in Somnolent is one of those five crowns**. Drisdin is identified in the vision as their maker.

Elgira and her four generals wore the crowns to channel the life and time offered by the rebel army during the Sundering. What began as a planned, limited sacrifice ended in catastrophic loss of life when the ritual's power was channelled into the Heart of Vaelond.

The party has encountered **one identified crown**, carried out of Somnolent by the escaping Perfidian fanatic. The present locations and operational powers of all five crowns remain unknown. The vision establishes a historical total of five but does not establish which other crowns still exist or who holds them.

## Clarified artefact count â€” Session 26

The canonical inventory for the Sundering is **ten objects: five Coronas of the Ashen Choir (called Crowns of Infinity in the memory) and five separate relics**. These are two groups, not ten Coronas or five items in total. The whereabouts of the other four Coronas remain unknown. See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 26 - Sundering Relics and the Market Brawl|Session 26]].

## Campaign record

- [[Session 12-14 - Fire, Faith and the Corona of the Ashen Choir]]

- [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Staff of Broken Time|Staff of Broken Time]] â€” another relic used during the ritual.
- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 25 - Ashes, Arrogance, and the Blood of Ages|Session 25]] â€” the party witnesses the crowns.

# Eye of Singularity

> [!summary] At a glance
> A named Ardynian object concealed in Elgira's robes in the shared memory of the Sundering. Its nature and current location are unknown.

## Known appearance

During the [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Tace Nox scroll|Tace Nox scroll]], Elgira notes that the **Eye of Singularity** lies within the folds of her robes, where it "opens and closes like a waking beast". The vision does not state what the Eye looks like beyond that image, or what role it served in the ritual.

## Unresolved

- Is the Eye a tool, living object or magical construct?
- Was it required for the Sundering or merely carried by Elgira?
- Does it survive, and who holds it now?

# Heart of Vaelond

> [!summary] At a glance
> An obsidian gem with a swirling green core, witnessed in Elgira's remembered Sundering ritual; its present location is unknown.

## The shared memory

In the [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Tace Nox scroll|Tace Nox scroll]], Elgira levitates a slender obsidian gem with a living green centre. She calls it the **Heart of Vaelond** and feeds into it the vast current gathered from herself and the five ritual crowns.

Elgira had calculated that volunteers would sacrifice a few years each. Once the magic entered the Heart, the process could not be stopped: **it drew away their entire lives**, killing thousands of volunteers. Elgira then completed the sealing of Ardynia using the gathered power. Afterward, the Heart was described as dark and hollow, its core empty.

## What remains unknown

- Whether the Heart still exists, and where it is.
- Whether its lethal excess was inherent, accidental or caused by a flaw in the ritual.
- Whether it can be used again and with what cost.
- How, if at all, it relates to the recovered Ardynian **Sanguis Aevi**.

# Ardynian Relics and Artefacts

A player-facing index of Ardynian objects and temporal materials known to the Defracturers. Some were recovered during expeditions to [[02 Places/Rakara/Tace Nox|Tace Nox]] and [[02 Places/Rakara/Rana Viam|Rana Viam]]; others were seen in the memory activated from the [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Tace Nox scroll|Tace Nox scroll]] in Session 25.

## Objects witnessed in the Sundering memory

The Sundering involved **ten objects in total: five Coronas of the Ashen Choir (the five Crowns of Infinity named in the vision) and five other relics**. Presence in the vision does not independently establish that all ten were necessary for the ritual or that every object survives today.

| Object witnessed | Identification / encounter | Current known location | Evidence of ritual function |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Five Coronas of the Ashen Choir / Crowns of Infinity** | The [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Corona of the Ashen Choir|Corona of the Ashen Choir]] encountered in Somnolent is **one** of the five | One Corona was carried away from Somnolent by Perfidian agents; whereabouts of the other crowns unknown | Originally designed to siphon magic and years of life; seen in the memory |
| [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Staff of Broken Time|Staff of Broken Time]] | **The same staff** Elgira used during the Sundering and the party encountered in [[02 Places/Rakara/Tace Nox|Tace Nox]] | Returned to Tace Nox; not currently held by the party | Wielded to direct the ritual |
| [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Heart of Vaelond|Heart of Vaelond]] | Obsidian focus with a green core | Unknown | Central to channeling the power; drew far more life than Elgira intended |
| [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Eye of Singularity|Eye of Singularity]] | Named object in Elgira's robes | Unknown | **Unconfirmed**: presence shown, function not established |
| Unidentified bracers | Seen on Elgira's wrists | Unknown | Role unknown; **not proven** to be the [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Bands of Distortion|Bands of Distortion]] |
| Unidentified chain | Worn around Elgira's neck | Unknown | Role unknown |

## Recovered or examined objects

- [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Bands of Distortion|Bands of Distortion]] â€” obtained at Rana Viam, with a connected heart stone; its powers were partly identified by Elmwood.
- [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Ring of Mind Shielding|Ring of Mind Shielding]] â€” recovered at Tace Nox and inhabited by a soul calling itself Din.
- [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Rana Viam scroll|Rana Viam scroll]] â€” **still unopened**.
- [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Tace Nox scroll|Tace Nox scroll]] â€” **activated**, with the complete Elgira memory retained on its page.
- [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Time Juice|Time Juice]] â€” green temporal substance identified as probable **Sanguis Aevi** (*Blood of Ages*).

## Session 26 â€” custody and possible anchors

The Defracturers retained the [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Bands of Distortion|Bands of Distortion]] with their heart stone; attunement remains undecided. The heart stone has led them to **suspect**, not establish, that other relics may have associated anchors. They may search Tace Nox for a possible tether to the [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Staff of Broken Time|Staff of Broken Time]]. The historical total remains **five Coronas plus five other relics**, with no additional object implied by this theory.

See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 26 - Sundering Relics and the Market Brawl|Session 26]].

# Rana Viam scroll

> [!summary] At a glance
> A **sealed Ardynian scroll** recovered during the Defracturers' exploration of [[02 Places/Rakara/Rana Viam|Rana Viam]]. Its message, function and method of activation remain unknown in the record through Session 26.

## Discovery and identification

The Defracturers recovered a sealed Ardynian scroll during the ruin expedition, alongside their encounter with the golem and the [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Bands of Distortion|Bands of Distortion]]. The record does not supply a transcription of its writing or confirm that it has been opened.

**This is not the scroll recovered from [[02 Places/Rakara/Tace Nox|Tace Nox]].** The separate [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Tace Nox scroll|Tace Nox scroll]] was activated by Elmwood Bluevine in Session 25 with probable Sanguis Aevi and displayed Elgira's memory.

## Current state â€” after Session 26

| Field | Recorded status |
|---|---|
| **Provenance** | Rana Viam, Clan Orvak |
| **Condition** | Sealed / unopened |
| **Content** | Unknown |
| **Function** | Unknown |
| **Current holder / storage location** | **Not explicitly established** in the supplied session notes |
| **Connection to the Tace Nox scroll** | Both are Ardynian, but no shared function has been demonstrated |

## Unresolved

- What does the scroll contain, and what would activate it?
- Does it concern the Bands, the Chronarchs or another subject entirely?
- Who currently has physical custody of it?

# Ring of Mind Shielding

> [!summary] At a glance
> A ring recovered from [[02 Places/Rakara/Tace Nox|Tace Nox]], currently worn by [[03 People/Player Characters/Ballar Novis|Ballar Novis]] and inhabited by a soul calling itself [[03 People/Notable Figures/Din|Din]].

## Discovery

Ballar found the ring inside one of the stone ottomans in Tace Nox. He equipped it without initially telling the rest of the party and had fully attuned to it by the time the group returned to Somnolent.

The ring was one of the objects that successfully left Tace Nox. This distinguishes it from the [[Staff of Broken Time|Staff of Broken Time]] and the emeralds taken from the ruin's guardian, which vanished or disintegrated when the party crossed the site's threshold.

## The soul within

A consciousness within the ring later began speaking privately to Ballar. It calls itself **Din**.

Din has:

- questioned Ballar about the party's location, destination and purpose;
- asked Ballar to help find it a body;
- offered to guide the group through Ardynian ruins where it is able to do so.

Ballar has not accepted Din's proposed arrangement, although their conversation remains open.

## Known properties

The chronicle establishes that:

- the ring contains or preserves a communicating soul;
- Din can speak directly to Ballar while he wears the ring;
- the ring remained in Ballar's possession after leaving Tace Nox.

The full magical properties of the ring have not been systematically recorded in the campaign chronicle.

> [!question] Unresolved
> - Who was Din before entering the ring?
> - How and when was Din's soul bound to it?
> - What kind of body does Din want?
> - What guidance can Din provide within Ardynian ruins?
> - Can Din communicate with anyone other than the ring's wearer?
> - What would happen if the ring changed hands?

# Staff of Broken Time

The **Staff of Broken Time** is an Ardynian relic found within Tace Nox. The party recovered it from a column of temporal energy and used it during the battle against the ruin's corrupted relics.

When the group crossed the threshold to leave, the staff vanished and returned to the ruin. Tace Nox appears unwilling to allow the artefact to be removed by ordinary means.

## Campaign record

- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 04 - Tace Nox|Session 04 - Tace Nox]]

## Identification in Session 25

The staff carried by Elgira in the shared Sundering memory from the [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Tace Nox scroll|Tace Nox scroll]] is **the Staff of Broken Time** that the Defracturers saw and handled in Tace Nox. This confirms that the artefact was part of the Sundering ritual. Its known status remains unchanged: it returned to Tace Nox when the party attempted to leave the ruin with it.

See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 25 - Ashes, Arrogance, and the Blood of Ages|Session 25]].

## Session 26 â€” possible key to removal

The party discussed the **possibility** that a keystone or anchor, comparable to the Bands of Distortionâ€™s heart stone, might still lie in [[02 Places/Rakara/Tace Nox|Tace Nox]] and explain why the staff returned there. No such object has been located or proven to exist for the staff. Recovering the staff remains unresolved. See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 26 - Sundering Relics and the Market Brawl|Session 26]].

# Tace Nox scroll

> [!info] Unabridged vision
> Recovered from [[02 Places/Rakara/Tace Nox|Tace Nox]] in Session 4, the scroll was deciphered by [[03 People/Notable Figures/Elmwood Bluevine|Elmwood Bluevine]] and activated in Session 25 using a sample of green temporal reagent. The Defracturers and Elmwood then experienced the following memory through Elgira's perspective. The account below is preserved as the full player handout; it records what the witnesses experienced, not a change to the accepted-history article.

The reagent was identified by Elmwood as likely **Sanguis Aevi** (*Blood of Ages*), the Ardynian term he had been seeking. The memory features the five **Crowns of Infinity**, the **Heart of Vaelond**, the **Eye of Singularity**, an unidentified chain and bracers, and a staff the party can identify as the **Staff of Broken Time**. One of the five crowns is the relic already encountered in Somnolent as the **Corona of the Ashen Choir**.

## The memory (full text)

![[Image1.png]]

Your body rearticulates itself as you appear in a large barn, remembering its shape one piece at a time. For a heartbeat you are nothing but pain, light, and the taste of iron. Then the world slams back into place around you.

Tears are welling in your eyes, but you strain, holding them back. It had to be done you think to yourself, now is not the time for weakness. Your jaw clenches tight, your grip nearly snapping the pommel of the dagger in your hand.

You look down at the dagger, dizzy as the adrenaline courses through your system, and watch a single drop of blood trickle down the blade and land on the floor with a dull wet thud. With a wince you wipe the bloody blade on your tunic and tuck it back inside its sheath.

Your fellow deserters sit around the table, eagerly awaiting your input, voices overlap with notes of excitement, trepidation and grim resolve, but it all blends into one overwhelming din as you struggle to right your mind. With a taste like ashes in your mouth you turn away from the crowd.

Walking to the corner of the barn you slowly pull a yellow and purple flower from your pocket, placing it reverently in a small pot. You allow yourself a moment of pride at how absurdly alive it is after all these years.

A thin man with short dark hair places a hand on your shoulder "An Ashbright in bloom, now there is something you don't see very often".

"Depends where you look, Vaelen." You smile weakly "But I don't think we'll see another in the wild in our lifetime". You shrug his hand from your shoulder and walk towards the table. The gathered voices slow to a hush, hanging on your next word.

![[Image2.png]]

When none comes, a woman in resplendent golden armour makes herself heard, eyeing you cautiously. "Drisdin?" She asks, the question itself unsaid.

You swallow hard. "Drisdin is gone" you manage through gritted teeth "the path is clear".

The woman looks at you distrustfully, measuring you for a second before asking again. "He is dead? He was the only one whoâ€¦"

"It is done, Marra" you snap with agitation, massaging your finger as you cut her off "I do not leave these things to chance".

You turn to two men seated to your right, a sombre looking cleric with a distinguished grey beard and an impudent man, kicking his feet up on the table. "You have 3 months. We will need every willing body for this to work."

The impudent man twirls a dagger around his fingers, letting it rotate round each finger twice before descending and repeating on the next one down as he grins "Oh don't worry, Vaelond is itching to see Ardynia fall. Desmon and I will barely need to lift a finger." He chuckles, nudging the cleric.

The cleric does not laugh as he replies grimly "Erith jests, as is his wont. But we will bring the flock".

You take a deep breath, before pulling your satchel from your shoulder and emptying it noisily onto the table. Five golden crowns infused with Sanguis Aevi spill out before them, veins of green pulsing beneath metal.

The bracers at your wrists tighten. The chain hanging from your neck burns cold. Somewhere within the folds of your robes, the Eye of Singularity opens and closes like a waking beast.

"Now," your voice sounds far away, even to yourself "let us end this".

_____
![[Image3.png]]
You step out of a portal into a wide expanse of greenery. In front of you sits the towering, imposing seat of Ardynia. A twisting, glittering myriad of impossible structures that laughs in the face of gods, in the face of physics. You take a moment to steel your resolve, clenching your fists and releasing them as you take a deep breath, knowing this is the last time you will ever see your home one way or another. "We were so brilliant," you mutter to yourself "so arrogant."

You lift one of the crowns, Drisdin's final masterpiece, and place it on your head before turning to the sea of people gathered behind you. Taking a shaky step to turn and face them, you slam your staff into the ground, using thaumaturgy to project the sound of the staff and your voice.

"I am sorry for what I must ask of you. I am sorry for the burden you must bear, to fix the horrors Ardynia has wrought. If I could find another way, to unwrap the knots in time I have tied, I would." A single tear breaks down your cheek as you look up, seeing several portals opening up in the field.

"I know the atrocities my people have inflicted on Vaelond, and I carry the responsibility of making so many of them possible. The Green Ascendancy, the Entropy Wars, the Permutation Siphonâ€¦ all stem from my folly." Your voice rises, growing in confidence as you see thousands of bodies appearing through the portals. "It matters not what my intentions were, I was too ignorant, too blinded by my own righteousness, too enamoured by my own intellect, to think about the cost."

You pause, the words tasting like ashes in your mouth. "I only asked whether I could. I called that wisdom."

"You have seen Ardynia's greed and ambition. Their insatiable appetite reached even the dragons. Where will it turn next? To the giants, the dwarves, the elves? To you? Who will come to your aid then?"

"Rhozka, Ulvarn, Orun, Ashka, Veltrin, the Sea-Mother and Moon-Father, and all the others? They abandoned you as you prayed at their feet for their compassion. They answered with silence whilst the Ardynian engines drank the years from your children. They watched contently, too focussed on Ardynian offerings to think about the rest of their flock. Those cruel, heartless beings, who encouraged ambition. Encouraged hubris. Encouraged progress at any cost!"

You feel the first drop of precipitation hit your cheek, a storm brewing that quickly morphs to hail, bouncing of your armour with a bright clang. Good, you think to yourself, letting a wry smile break your lips, let the gods bear witness to the power of Vaelond.

"We will take that hubris and use it against them." You slam your staff into the ground, letting the noise reverberate for an uneasy second. "These Crowns," you point to yourself and the other four wearing them "The Crowns of Infinity. Designed to enslave. Designed to siphon magic from those under their thrall and use it to power Ardyniaâ€™s immortality. They were made to sacrifice those lesser people, you, your friends, your family and all your futures, to push Ardynia's present to new heights."

You smile coldly "It never crossed their minds, that so others would willingly sacrifice their future to give the next generation, your children, the opportunity to live their lives in freedom. That selflessness, that courage, that altruism is alien to them!"

![[Image4.png]]

You had calculated the thresholds. Just a few years given by each supporter would be more than enough. An old orc kneels first. Then a woman in grey tunic. Then a boy no older than sixteen. Hands rise across the field. Blades are drawn.

Turning to face Ardynia, you raise your staff to the sky, your eyes narrowing in deep thought as you mutter an incantation. The world around you is bathed in luminescence, green-grey tendrils reaching from your crown to its four siblings, splitting off into a web of new strands, towards the army of thousands.

"Give no more than you want, but give everything you can to banish this scourge from the face of Vaelond!" You scream. You feel the power flowing through you, pumping like blood through the beams which connect the crowd to the crowns and the crowns to you. You hear a hundred rallying cries, in a dozen languages and a thousand voices, the sounds slamming against each other like a wave.

The power surges and begins to coalesce around your fingers, streams of magic intertwining like vines. The sheer ferocity of the magic you hold starts to manifest around you, lifting you into the air. As you concentrate on folding the power in on itself you look back across the crowd, your generals.

They are the first to understand - Vaelen, Erith, Desmon and Marra are fighting against the current, trying to stem the flow of energy into you, their teeth gritted, sweat pouring from their brows.

Marra's face is not afraid. It is accusing. Vaelen braces both hands against his crown as if he could tear it from his skull. Desmon is screaming a prayer into a world that has stopped listening. Worst of all, Erith wears a mask of grim determination, the first time you've ever seen him without a smile.

![[Image5.png]]

With a final calming breath you open your palm, a slender but perfectly formed obsidian gem with a swirling green core lifts itself from your skin, the Heart of Vaelond. With every ounce of concentration you have, you direct the power towards the centre of the gem, a trickle at first. The moment that first sliver makes contact, everything changes.

Rain hangs unfallen in the sky in front of you, the roar of your army stretches out into one long, endless low note as you hang in that epoch between heartbeats, a step outside time. The trickle gains momentum, becoming a stream, then a flood. You feel it surge through you, feel the shock from every man and woman in the ritual as the Heart siphons every ounce of power from them.

You try to scream, to stop the flow, but it is futile. You have already stepped too far out of time. You feel your body rising to the heavens as your supporters crumble into dust. Thousands gone, massacred in an instant. You feel their pain, their terror, as their very life force courses through you into the heart.

They offered years, but the Heart takes lives. You realise with a solemn resignation.

Blinking back tears you turn all your resolve to the task at hand, promising yourself that their sacrifice will not be in vain.

The heart buzzes as you push the final strands of power into its core. There is a pause, a brief moment of nothing as you feel your generals inch towards you, to look upon the core. A silent, brilliantly bright wave of green light pulses out from the heart in every direction. The light takes on mass, the pure energy obliterating the ground beneath you leaving behind a mile wide crater. The power swirls around the Heart like a hurricane, forcing you to close your eyes.

You feel the universe creak, a sickening snap which you feel in your soul rather than hear. When you open your eyes it is gone. Ardynia, the glittering empire, is no more. There is no smoke, no ruins. Just a mountain range where your home once stood.

You turn to your generals, your friends, and slowly speak "It'sâ€¦ it's over". But there is no sound. Vaelen is looking at you, or at least where you are. He is looking through you. You blink, staring down at your hand, the Heart of Vaelond sits there, hollow and dark, its core empty.

Your veins are aching in your wrists, and you notice the change. Green like Sanguis Aevi. The blood of ages, the blood of gods.

Vaelen whispers your name, as if it is something monstrous. You do not hear him, not truly. The sound arrives inside you, an echo inside your bones, as the stasis fades and the rain begins to fall again.

## Later interpretation â€” Session 26

The party distinguishes **five Coronas of the Ashen Choir (the Crowns of Infinity depicted above)** from **five other relics**, for ten Sundering objects total. They also suspect a possible keystone or anchor associated with the Staff of Broken Time at Tace Nox, by analogy with the Bands of Distortion and their heart stone. **This remains conjecture and does not amend the memoryâ€™s unabridged text.** See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 26 - Sundering Relics and the Market Brawl|Session 26]].

## Campaign and relic references

- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 25 - Ashes, Arrogance, and the Blood of Ages|Session 25]]
- [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Corona of the Ashen Choir|Corona of the Ashen Choir]] â€” one of the five Crowns of Infinity shown in the memory.
- [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Staff of Broken Time|Staff of Broken Time]] â€” the staff Elgira is seen carrying and wielding.
- [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Heart of Vaelond|Heart of Vaelond]] â€” the obsidian focus whose siphoning exceeded Elgira's calculation.
- [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Eye of Singularity|Eye of Singularity]] â€” concealed among Elgira's robes; purpose unknown.
- [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Time Juice|Time Juice]] â€” substance likely corresponding to Sanguis Aevi.
- [[01 Welcome to Vaelond/The Sundering of Ardynia|The Sundering of Ardynia]] â€” the accepted historical account; unchanged.

# Time Juice

> [!summary] At a glance
> The Defracturers' informal name for a green Ardynian substance associated with temporal machinery in Tace Nox and Rana Viam.

## Appearance and discovery

The substance appears as a green fluid or residue within Ardynian ruins.

When the party entered [[02 Places/Rakara/Rana Viam|Rana Viam]], Ieuan recognised residue in a central basin as the same material previously encountered at [[02 Places/Rakara/Tace Nox|Tace Nox]].

The name **Time Juice** is a party nickname. In Session 25, Elmwood identified a reagent mentioned in Ardynian research as **Sanguis Aevi**, meaning *Blood of Ages*, and recognised the party's sample as probably the same material. This is his strong identification, not a full alchemical analysis.

## Rana Viam samples

Within Rana Viam, the party found:

- a beaker containing the substance;
- alchemical equipment from which n'Dong extracted two additional vials;
- a central basin designed to receive the fluid.

n'Dong poured some Time Juice into the basin. Green light spread across the adjoining door in a web-like pattern and opened the passage into the crossing chamber.

## Known function

Time Juice has directly activated at least two different Ardynian mechanisms: the doorway in Rana Viam and, in Session 25, the sealed Tace Nox scroll when a drop was applied to its seal.

Its wider connection to time magic is strongly suggested by:

- its presence in Chronarch ruins;
- its familiar green appearance;
- the temporal mechanisms and relics found at those sites.

The substance has not been deliberately consumed or applied to a living creature in the recorded sessions.

## Current status

The party recovered multiple samples but used some to open the Rana Viam passage. The quantity remaining has not been recorded.

> [!question] Unresolved
> - What is Time Juice made from, and how precisely does it relate to Sanguis Aevi?
> - Was it a fuel, reagent, coolant or stabilising substance?
> - Does every Chronarch site use the same material?
> - Can it affect creatures, objects or the passage of time directly?
> - Is it safe to transport?
> - How much remains in the party's possession?

## Campaign references

- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 17 - Shrieks, Widows and Time Juice|Session 17]]

## Session 25 â€” Blood of Ages

At the Crooked Hat, Elmwood found that a drop of the recovered green reagent activated the [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Tace Nox scroll|Tace Nox scroll]], drawing the Defracturers and Elmwood into a shared memory of the Sundering. The scroll describes the five ritual crowns as infused with Sanguis Aevi and Elgira's veins turning green after the ritual; neither observation explains how the reagent is produced.

See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 25 - Ashes, Arrogance, and the Blood of Ages|Session 25]].

# Dragon's Dandruff

> [!summary] At a glance
> A flaky amber salt traditionally used as seasoning by lizard-folk, now linked to Rhul Slowdrift's fighting-pit operation in Dazuun.

## Description

Dragon's Dandruff is a flaky amber salt traditionally used as seasoning by lizard-folk. Its signature **chilli-high** makes any party memorable, though one unnamed individual unused to the taste described it as akin to chewing on a hot coal.

In Session 21, [[03 People/Notable Deceased/Ulsar Quinn|Ulsar Quinn]] revealed that three [[02 Places/Rakara/Clans/Clan Khaali|Khaali]] alchemists are producing Dragon's Dandruff for use by contestants in the fighting pits of [[02 Places/Rakara/Voruun/Dazuun|Dazuun]].

## Effects

A teaspoon of Dragon's Dandruff causes an initial burning sizzle, dealing **1d4 radiant damage**.

Afterwards, the consumer experiences a rushing sensation of joy, increasing **Charisma by 1 for one hour**.

Once the salt metabolises, the consumer becomes flooded with adrenaline. For one hour, their **Strength, Constituion and Dexterity increase by 2**, and they feel compelled to undertake physical tasks.

After the effects fade, the consumer suffers **1 level of exhaustion**.

The recorded effects do **not stack**; this statement does not by itself settle addiction, withdrawal, repeated-use timing or the DM's choice of ability-score cap.

Undead creatures take **1d8 radiant damage for every teaspoon consumed**.

## Current significance

Dragon's Dandruff is now tied to the [[04 Organisations/Broken Fang|Broken Fang's]] Rakaran operations. [[03 People/Notable Figures/Rhul Slowdrift|Rhul Slowdrift]] has taken control of the fighting pits of Dazuun, and the stimulant appears to be part of that control.

> [!question] Unresolved
> - Are the pit contestants taking Dragon's Dandruff willingly?
> - How addictive or dangerous is repeated use?
> - Are the Khaali alchemists working under duress, for profit or for political reasons?
> - Where exactly is the lab south of Somnolent?

## Recovered samples

After Ulsar Quinn's camp was destroyed, [[03 People/Player Characters/n'Dong|n'Dong]] searched [[03 People/Notable Deceased/Talan Rook|Talan Rook]]'s tent and recovered **two vials of Dragon's Dandruff**.

The party therefore now possesses physical samples of the substance rather than relying solely on Ulsar's description of Rhul's supply chain.

## Session 26 â€” Karessa and the fighters

[[03 People/Mentioned Figures/Yama and Spopovich|Yama and Spopovich]], two former Dazuun pit fighters, appeared visibly affected by Dragonâ€™s Dandruff in Somnolent. They told [[03 People/Player Characters/n'Dong|n'Dong]] that their supplier was **[[03 People/Notable Figures/Karessa Val|Karessa]]**, a Khaali woman identified by three fingers. They also alleged a connection between booking agent [[03 People/Mentioned Figures/Oruc|Oruc]] and Rhul Slowdriftâ€™s match-fixing.

At that point, Karessaâ€™s identity as a producer and the labâ€™s location remained unverified. Both were established in Session 27. nâ€™Dong gave the fighters more of the substance and promised further supplies in exchange for information; consequently, the **number of vials now in his possession cannot be inferred** from the two recovered in Session 22. See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 26 - Sundering Relics and the Market Brawl|Session 26]].

## Session 27 â€” the production laboratory

The burned One Ball map led the party to [[02 Places/Rakara/Dragon's Dandruff Laboratory|the laboratory]]. The alchemists were [[03 People/Notable Figures/Karessa Val|Karessa Val]], [[03 People/Notable Figures/Pella|Pella]] and [[03 People/Notable Figures/Davos|Davos]]. Quota material and a threatening message were found; Ieuan destroyed the recovered recipe. The remaining stock reportedly amounted to roughly two-thirds of the next shipment, valued at 5,000â€“6,000 gp on the street. Karessa attributed threats against families to Rhul and Prodosius. Four guards fell but the lab, remaining stock and alchemists were still at the scene. nâ€™Dong also gave the former pit fighters further doses; the amount remaining in his possession is unknown.

See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

# Horses

The party's five mounts, as recorded in the campaign tracker and Session 15. The rider assignments below are the confirmed repository canon.

## Bracken

![[Bracken.png|260]]

**Recorded rider:** [[03 People/Player Characters/Enrak Kiran|Enrak Kiran]]

**D&D Beyond sheet:** [Bracken](https://www.dndbeyond.com/monsters/6298820-bracken)

Slow but exceptionally steady. Bracken stood against an owlbear to protect Enrak.

## Cobb

![[Cobb.png|260]]

**Recorded rider:** [[03 People/Player Characters/Ieuan|Ieuan]]

**D&D Beyond sheet:** [Cobb](https://www.dndbeyond.com/monsters/6298828-cobb)

A dependable, calm and sturdy mount. The name later caused some amusement when the party met a farmer also called Cobb.

## Vesper

![[Vesper.png|260]]

**Recorded rider:** [[03 People/Player Characters/Erelmor|Erelmor]]

**D&D Beyond sheet:** [Vesper](https://www.dndbeyond.com/monsters/6298790-vesper)

An elegant dark horse suited to stealth and grace. Vesper fled the scorpion battle carrying an unconscious Erelmor to safety.

## Nail

![[Nail.png|260]]

**Recorded rider:** [[03 People/Player Characters/n'Dong|n'Dong]]

**D&D Beyond sheet:** [Nail](https://www.dndbeyond.com/monsters/6298832-nail)

A former courier horse: quick, enduring and somewhat shy when battle approaches.

## Kingcup

![[Kingcup.png|260]]

**Recorded rider:** [[03 People/Player Characters/Ballar Novis|Ballar Novis]]

**D&D Beyond sheet:** [Kingcup](https://www.dndbeyond.com/monsters/6298836-kingcup)

Beautiful, vain, difficult and more suited to a parade ground than a battlefield; an immediate match for Ballar.

## Current situation

**The five mounts returned to Somnolent with the Defracturers in Session 25 and were stabled before the party went back to the Bastion.** The chronicle does not name the stable or record a later relocation through Session 26. See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 25 - Ashes, Arrogance, and the Blood of Ages|Session 25]].

After the destruction of Ulsar's camp, Ballar spent time soothing [[03 People/Player Characters/Ballar Novis|Ballar's]] difficult mare Kingcup. Session 22 marked one of the first moments in which she appeared close to not regarding him with contempt; during the return journey in Session 23, grooming earned what the chronicle describes as perhaps the most genuine affection she had yet shown him.

By the end of Session 23, the party and its mounts were only a few hours from Somnolent; the actual return and stabling took place in Session 25.

# Open Threads

A record of the Defracturers' outstanding objectives and the consequences of earlier choices, current through **Session 27**.

- **Active:** Pursued, discussed or materially advanced in recent sessions.
- **Inactive:** Unresolved but not currently being pursued.
- **Offscreen:** Actions or consequences unfolding without the party's direct involvement.
- **Closed:** Objectives completed, decisively failed or superseded. A closed objective can still lead to another open thread.

**Urgency** is a rough guide to immediacy, not a requirement that the party pursue a particular plot. **Last development** means the most recent relevant event documented in the campaign reference, not necessarily the last time the thread was mentioned. Where a lead is unverified, it is presented as a lead rather than an established fact.

## Active threads

### Recover Job and the missing watch

**Urgency:** 5 â€” Very high  
**Theme:** Somnolent / Stone Pass

**Objective:** Locate Job and the other watch members and rescue any survivors.

**Current position:** Gayle escaped the undead ambush at [[02 Places/Rakara/Stone Pass|Stone Pass]]; Job and his companions held back the attackers. Their condition is unknown. Romayne requested help, but the party went to the laboratory first.

**Key history:**
- Gayle reached the Bastion injured and described the encounter after Mariasz healed her.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

---

### Secure the alchemists and their families

**Urgency:** 4 â€” High  
**Theme:** Broken Fang / Karessa

**Objective:** Determine protection and evacuation arrangements for the alchemists and their families.

**Current position:** Karessa and Pella cooperated against the guards; Davos appeared willing to leave. Proposed staged deaths and alternative employment are not final; no family has been secured.

**Key history:**
- Karessa reported threats from Rhul and Prodosius; Pella was reluctant to leave a profitable operation.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

---

### Resolve obligations to Somnolent

**Urgency:** 4 â€” High  
**Theme:** Patronage / Bastion

**Objective:** Clarify staff requisition, conduct, assignments and custody of relics with Romayne.

**Current position:** Romayne confronted the party over staff use, Terrickâ€™s assault and dangerous artefacts. No formal suspension or termination occurred.

**Key history:**
- Alaaric cited the existing contract; Ballar voluntarily entered and then left the Shackles.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

---

### Find Eden Carrion and Summer Quaresh

**Urgency:** 5 â€” Very high  
**Theme:** Anthea Bloodclot / Loyal Crows

**Objective:** Locate [[03 People/Notable Figures/Eden Carrion|Eden Carrion]] and [[03 People/Notable Figures/Summer Quaresh|Summer Quaresh]] and establish their precise involvement in [[03 People/Notable Deceased/Alistair Prusset|Alistair Prusset's]] death.

**Current position:** Gayle is recovering and reported a sighting at [[02 Places/Rakara/Stone Pass|Stone Pass]]. Both fugitives appeared pale when Job approached; undead then surrounded the watch. Their current location and role are unknown.

**Key history:**
- Eden and Summer fled Somnolent after Prusset was killed while in custody. Their presence and suspected involvement do not, by themselves, establish who struck the fatal blow.
- [[03 People/Bastion Staff/Caelen Briar|Caelen Briar]] and [[03 People/Bastion Staff/Gudren Flint|Gudren Flint]] matched an unconfirmed sighting to an area where old records describe naturally dampened magic. Their research did **not** confirm the fugitives' exact whereabouts.
- Gayle and Job left Somnolent to investigate that lead.
- Gayle returned alone, injured and unconscious in Session 26; her account followed in Session 27.

- Gayle described the ambush in Session 27; a separate rescue thread concerns Job and the watch.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

---

### Find Prodosius and confront the Broken Fang

**Urgency:** 3 â€” Medium  
**Theme:** Broken Fang / n'Dong

**Objective:** Trace the Broken Fang's operations through [[03 People/Notable Figures/Rhul Slowdrift|Rhul Slowdrift]] towards [[03 People/Notable Figures/Prodosius|Prodosius]], whom n'Dong seeks for the harm done to him and his family.

**Current position:** The party has reached [[02 Places/Rakara/Dragon's Dandruff Laboratory|the laboratory]]; Karessa alleges threats by Rhul and Prodosius. The Dazuun and Oruc leads remain open.

**Key history:**
- The Broken Fang have expanded their activities into Rakara; the party destroyed lieutenant Ulsar Quinn's camp.
- Ulsar described Rhul's control of the fighting pits of [[02 Places/Rakara/Voruun/Dazuun|Dazuun]] as a major part of his Rakaran operation.
- [[03 People/Notable Figures/Antoinne Cassan|Antoinne Cassan]] used the [[02 Places/Rakara/Somnolent/One Ball Inn|One Ball Inn]] in connection with Broken Fang fencing and contraband.
- In Session 26, former pit fighters Yama and Spopovich alleged that Oruc arranged fights and received payment from Rhul to fix matches.

- Four laboratory guards were defeated, but the operation has not been destroyed.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

---

### Identify the remaining Loyal Crows

**Urgency:** 3 â€” Medium  
**Theme:** Anthea Bloodclot / Loyal Crows

**Objective:** Establish the surviving members, associates and objectives of [[04 Organisations/Loyal Crows|Anthea's Loyal Crows]].

**Current position:** Investigate the three existing research trails and the undead ambush at [[02 Places/Rakara/Stone Pass|Stone Pass]]. No new Crow identity or link between Eula/Galt and the ambush is established.

**Key history:**
- **Known Crows:** Eden Carrion and Summer Quaresh.
- **Tessa Betu (Erzhin):** a stonecutter and scholar who reportedly met Eden; the record suggests an unexplained possible contact with Father Zantos.
- **Arun Yev (Temir):** a scholar linked to Summer who subsequently rose through the Wavecutters. Reports of possible future leadership are speculative.
- **Eula and Galt (formerly of Voruun):** figures linked to necromancy and repeated private meetings with Eden and Summer; their actual allegiance is unknown.
- Anthea's recovered note refers to three dead Crows and only Anthea and Eden remaining at that time. How that statement fits Summer's membership remains unresolved.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

---

### Locate the Sundering relics and determine their fate

**Urgency:** 3 â€” Medium  
**Theme:** Ardynia / Sundering

**Objective:** Locate and understand the artefacts used in the Sundering, then decide whether they should be recovered, secured or otherwise dealt with.

**Current position:** Romayne requested review and possible secure custody in Alaaricâ€™s safe. The party has not handed over any relics; the Bands remain unattuned, Rana Viam scroll unopened and Staff anchor unproven.

**Key history:**
- At Tace Nox, the party found the Staff but could not remove it; they retained a scroll subsequently activated by Elmwood.
- At Rana Viam, they recovered the Bands, a heart stone and a separate unopened scroll.
- The Tace Nox scroll revealed Elgira's memory of the Sundering and **ten ritual objects: five Coronas and five other artefacts**.
- The heart stone suggests a possible anchor mechanism, but no equivalent anchor has been found for the Staff. The party has not agreed whether gathering all these dangerous objects is desirable.

- Romayne raised custody of dangerous relics in Session 27.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

---

### Dismantle the Dragon's Dandruff operation

**Urgency:** 5 â€” Very high  
**Theme:** Broken Fang / Dragon's Dandruff

**Objective:** Disable the production site and determine how to prevent production resuming and interrupt the supply to Dazuun.

**Current position:** The farm on the One Ball map is [[02 Places/Rakara/Dragon's Dandruff Laboratory|the laboratory]]. Four guards have fallen, but the alchemists and remaining stock are still there and the laboratory is intact.

**Key history:**
- Ulsar Quinn described three Khaali alchemists operating south of Somnolent and supplying the fighting pits.
- The party found a partially burned map to a farm and its rear barn in the cellar beneath the burned One Ball Inn.
- Yama and Spopovich identified Karessa as their source and said the drug was widespread among Dazuun fighters.
- These were initially separate leads; following the map in Session 27 confirmed the farm as the laboratory and Karessa as one of its alchemists.

- The party identified Karessa, Pella and Davos; Ieuan destroyed the recipe, and staged deaths were proposed.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

---

### Find the Old Grey Pike

**Urgency:** 2 â€” Low  
**Theme:** Erelmor / Ashenhom

**Objective:** Find [[03 People/Notable Figures/Ervain Megbanas|Ervain Megbanas]], the Old Grey Pike, and uncover his actual role in the destruction of [[02 Places/Doro/Ashenhom|Ashenhom]] and the death of Erelmor's family.

**Current position:** Erelmor now knows Ervain left Somnolent. Alaaric confirmed he informed Mariasz before the One Ball fire. Ervainâ€™s whereabouts are unknown.

**Key history:**
- Ervain is a disgraced former Doroan commander whose reputation was damaged by Ashenhom's destruction; his direct responsibility for the murders remains uncertain.
- He gambled at the One Ball Inn, lost money to Ballar and displayed hostility towards Doro.
- Alaaric informed the Bastion of his departure during Session 24, without giving a destination.
- Erelmor's desire for vengeance remains, but his confidence was shaken by the events of Session 26.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

---

### Understand the Church of Elgira's intentions

**Urgency:** 1 â€” Very low  
**Theme:** Perfid / Church of Elgira / Anthea Bloodclot

**Objective:** Establish why the Church targeted Anthea and what it intends to do with its recovered Ardynian artefact.

**Current position:** No immediate Church-focused investigation is being pursued. The known links between the assassination, the Rootbound and the [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Corona of the Ashen Choir|Corona of the Ashen Choir]] remain the main leads.

**Key history:**
- Evidence uncovered by the party indicates that Perfidian interests, acting through Prusset, hired a Rootbound cell to murder Anthea Bloodclot.
- Perfidian operatives recovered a Corona of the Ashen Choir from Somnolent.
- The Session 25 vision revealed a far more complicated account of Elgira, the saints and the Sundering than public Church doctrine.
- These discoveries have not established the Church's complete present-day plans.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 25 - Ashes, Arrogance, and the Blood of Ages|Session 25]] (new Sundering context).

---

### Assess the rumoured Doroâ€“Perfid peace initiative

**Urgency:** 1 â€” Very low  
**Theme:** Doro / Perfid / House Nirrell

**Objective:** Discover whether peace negotiations are actually being pursued, and how they might affect [[02 Places/Doro/Noble Houses/House Nirrell|House Nirrell]] and Ballar's family.

**Current position:** Ballar has heard unverified reports at the Doro embassy. No negotiations, agreement or formal policy have been confirmed.

**Key history:**
- The reports suggest Nirrell may be influential and that Ballar's father, [[03 People/Notable Figures/Xilpeiros Nirrell|Xilpeiros Nirrell]], could benefit politically.
- Trenhalde was reported to be broadly favourable; Aequinth and Bromere reportedly appeared less enthusiastic.
- Ballar's father also asked for his assessment of Somnolent's political situation.
- These rumours are distinct from the investigation into why the Church ordered Anthea's murder.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 26 - Sundering Relics and the Market Brawl|Session 26]].

---

### Investigate the One Ball Inn fire and Antoinne's disappearance

**Urgency:** 2 â€” Low  
**Theme:** Somnolent / Broken Fang

**Objective:** Establish what happened to [[03 People/Notable Figures/Antoinne Cassan|Antoinne Cassan]], who burned the [[02 Places/Rakara/Somnolent/One Ball Inn|One Ball Inn]], and how the creatures beneath it came to be there.

**Current position:** The burned map led to [[02 Places/Rakara/Dragon's Dandruff Laboratory|the laboratory]]; a slaad-like creature was killed en route. The cause of the fire, Antoinneâ€™s fate and origin of the cellar creatures remain unknown.

**Key history:**
- The One Ball served a Broken Fang-connected fencing operation; n'Dong and Erelmor killed Sunshine and Loup, men associated with that network.
- Antoinne disappeared, leaving a ransacked office with blood, coded transactions and missing ledger pages. No body was recovered.
- The inn then burned to the ground, and Session 25 revealed gibbering mouthers and slaad tadpoles in its cellar. Three trapped residents died and some tadpoles escaped.
- The mapped farm is a concrete lead, but its connection to the fire, the creatures or the Dragon's Dandruff operation remains uncertain.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

---

### Awaken Ieuan's Star Seed

**Urgency:** 1 â€” Very low  
**Theme:** Ieuan / Star Seed

**Objective:** Complete the remaining stage of understanding needed to awaken [[06 Library/Relics and Artefacts/Star Seed|the Star Seed]].

**Current position:** Ieuan is working on the **nature** aspect of the Seed. Meditation with [[03 People/Notable Figures/Father Zantos|Father Zantos]] in Session 26 did not complete the breakthrough.

**Key history:**
- Father Zantos gave Ieuan the Seed with the promise that it would bloom as he developed his druidic path.
- The earlier tracker records successful Religion and Arcana checks; the dedicated Star Seed article explicitly describes successful faith-based progress but does not independently document the Arcana result.
- Ieuan has struggled to connect with the Seed through nature, at times hearing [[Plague|Plague]] demand that he *spread*.
- Zantos encouraged him to reconnect patiently with his older connection to the earth.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 26 - Sundering Relics and the Market Brawl|Session 26]].

**Current position:** The party has significant clues but is not presently pursuing an individual saint's whereabouts.

**Key history:**
- Vaelen's preserved message at Rana Viam warned of conflict among the surviving saints and suggested he had prepared means of preserving himself.
- In Session 25, Elmwood identified Anthea Bloodclot as **Saint Marra** from the witnessed memory.
- n'Dong recognised the likeness of **Saint Erith** in a portrait of Minir's first Arc Master; this is strong evidence, though the details of Erith's later life are not fully established.

**Current position:** The journey was postponed when the party chose to return to Somnolent. Hushvale has not been explored during the campaign.

**Key history:**
- A farmer named Cobb described Hushvale as increasingly closed and unwelcoming, to the extent that he avoids travelling there.
- The party suspects local Rootbound influence, but the relevant people, faction and cause of the change are unconfirmed.

**Current position:** The wider conspiracy has been traced to Perfidian interests and a contracted Rootbound cell, but the direct killer and precise cell remain unidentified.

**Key history:**
- The investigation established Bloodspore as the likely poison used against Anthea.
- The party uncovered evidence connecting Prusset and Perfidian interests to a Rootbound assassination cell.

**Urgency:** Inactive  
**Theme:** Ardynia / Ballar

**Objective:** Establish the true identity and intentions of **Din**, the soul within Ballar's [[06 Library/Ardynian Relics and Artefacts/Ring of Mind Shielding|Ring of Mind Shielding]], and decide whether to help him obtain a body.

## Offscreen threads

### Revive Mireya Dinak

**Urgency:** 4 â€” High  
**Theme:** Bastion / Mireya Dinak

**Objective:** Restore [[03 People/Notable Figures/Mireya Dinak|Mireya Dinak]] to life, if an eligible spell and suitable material component are available.

**Current position:** Mireya remains dead. Ballar tasked Derrick with delivering her body and a diamond to Elmwood; delivery and restoration remain unconfirmed. Verify the diamond value and Gentle Repose timing before assuming the spell is eligible.

**Key history:**
- The Defracturers rescued Mireya from ogres who had killed her husband. She then watched the party's horses during their exploration of [[02 Places/Rakara/Rana Viam|Rana Viam]].
- [[03 People/Notable Deceased/Ulsar Quinn|Ulsar Quinn]] captured her during the Broken Fang's theft of the mounts. She died in the fire that consumed his camp.
- Ballar recovered her remains and cast *Gentle Repose* in Session 21, intending to secure the time and resources for resurrection.
- The chronicle does not establish whether preservation was renewed over the intervening weeks or whether either individual diamond meets the spell's component requirement. No resurrection has yet occurred.

- Derrick was given the task in Session 27; work now proceeds offscreen.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

- Broken vials labelled **"slaadpole"** suggest that some creatures had been contained or transported.
