### Await Malt's search for a Pot of Awakening

**Urgency:** 1 â€” Very low  
**Theme:** Bushtache / Ballar

**Objective:** Learn whether Malt can obtain a Pot of Awakening for **Bushtache**, the cabbage Ballar spoke to.

**Current position:** Malt has been released to pursue the task; neither his whereabouts nor the outcome is recorded.

**Key history:**
- Ballar cast *Speak with Plants* on a cabbage that identified itself as Bushtache.
- Rather than retain Malt as a prisoner, Ballar spared him and sent him to find a Pot of Awakening for the cabbage.
- No return, delivery or further message from Malt has been recorded.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 22 - Bodies, Blight, and Bushtache|Session 22]].

---

### Determine what becomes of Plague in Eula's possession

**Urgency:** 3 â€” Medium  
**Theme:** Plague / Eula

**Objective:** Determine whether [[Plague|Plague]] spreads through the infected body Eula collected, and what Eula might do with it.

**Current position:** The location and condition of Salla's infected corpse are unknown. Plague has continued communicating with Ieuan, suggesting its influence is not confined to the cuttings he carried.

**Key history:**
- Ieuan planted one cutting of Plague in Salla's corpse, which Eula subsequently collected.
- Plague told Ieuan the cutting had found many bodies and opportunities, though the precise meaning is not established.
- Ieuan later planted his remaining cuttings, yet Plague's voice continued to reach him and interfered with his efforts to study the Star Seed.
- This thread concerns the **offscreen infected body**; Ieuan's continuing connection to Plague is a related unresolved personal consequence.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 23 - Ettins, Owlbears, and Betting Losses|Session 23]] (continuing voice); [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 21 - Contracts, Command, and a Burning Camp|Session 21]] (Eula collected the corpse).

## Closed threads

### Destroy the False Gardener's immediate corruption

**Urgency:** Closed  
**Theme:** Orvak / False Gardener

**Objective:** Eliminate the immediate source of corruption at Maelor Vey's ruined home.

**Current position:** **Completed.** The Defracturers defeated the dividing black ooze and destroyed the well from which it emerged. Oakrah believed the remaining local taint could be cleared.

**Key history:** The group investigated the False Gardener's old site after Oakrah confronted Ieuan. Destroying the source resolved that immediate expedition, **not necessarily Plague's wider survival or influence**.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 22 - Bodies, Blight, and Bushtache|Session 22]].

---

### Resolve Malt's imprisonment

**Urgency:** Closed  
**Theme:** Ballar / Bushtache

**Objective:** Decide what to do with Malt as a prisoner.

**Current position:** **Completed.** Ballar freed Malt on the condition that he seek a Pot of Awakening for Bushtache.

**Key history:** Malt's captivity was resolved; the **separate offscreen task** remains open until news of his mission arrives.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 22 - Bodies, Blight, and Bushtache|Session 22]].

---

### Destroy Ulsar Quinn's Broken Fang camp

**Urgency:** Closed  
**Theme:** Broken Fang

**Objective:** Defeat the Broken Fang force at Ulsar Quinn's camp.

**Current position:** **Completed.** The camp was destroyed, and Ulsar Quinn and Talan Rook were killed.

**Key history:** The attack also caused Mireya's death. Ballar later recovered and preserved her remains; **her resurrection is a separate active thread**.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 21 - Contracts, Command, and a Burning Camp|Session 21]].

---

### Recover the party's horses

**Urgency:** Closed  
**Theme:** Party resources / Broken Fang

**Objective:** Recover the horses seized by Ulsar's forces.

**Current position:** **Completed.** The party recovered its mounts and resumed travelling with them.

**Key history:** The horses were taken while the Defracturers explored Rana Viam, prompting their pursuit of Ulsar's Broken Fang unit.

**Last development:** By [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 23 - Ettins, Owlbears, and Betting Losses|Session 23]], the party was returning towards Somnolent with its horses.

---

### Identify the owner of the House Bromere favour coin

**Urgency:** Closed  
**Theme:** Doro / Loyal Crows

**Objective:** Trace the House Bromere favour coin connected to Buggie's orders.

**Current position:** **Completed.** The coin had belonged to Ervain Megbanas, who lost it to Eden Carrion in a dice game.

**Key history:** The coin connected the investigation into Buggie and Prusset with Doro's noble and military circles, although that ownership history does not establish a wider Bromere conspiracy.

**Last development:** Resolved in earlier Somnolent investigations; the original tracker does not identify the exact session.

---

### Find Buggie and establish why Prusset was targeted

**Urgency:** Closed  
**Theme:** Emberwake / Loyal Crows

**Objective:** Identify Buggie's employer and why Alistair Prusset was singled out.

**Current position:** **Completed.** Buggie disclosed that Eden Carrion hired his group to abduct Prusset and steal information.

**Key history:** The party met Buggie at Thistlewound, heard his account of the mercenary arrangement and acquired evidence leading into the broader Loyal Crows investigation.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 03 - Into Buggie's Den|Session 3]].

---

### Rescue Terrick Lowgins

**Urgency:** Closed  
**Theme:** Perfid / Bastion

**Objective:** Free [[03 People/Notable Figures/Terrick Lowgins|Terrick Lowgins]] from detention at the Perfidian embassy.

**Current position:** **Completed.** Terrick was rescued, and the former embassy later became the Defracturers' Bastion.

**Key history:** The embassy raid ended the party's immediate rescue objective but led to lasting patronage and diplomatic arrangements with Somnolent.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 16 - Cackles, Corpses and Catastrophe|Session 16]] (embassy and Bastion aftermath).

---

### Capture Alistair Prusset

**Urgency:** Closed  
**Theme:** Perfid / Anthea Bloodclot

**Objective:** Take Alistair Prusset into custody for questioning about the conspiracy against Anthea.

**Current position:** **Completed, with later fatal consequences.** Prusset was captured and interrogated, then killed while held in Somnolent custody.

**Key history:** His death and the escape of Eden and Summer subsequently became a separate active investigation; their precise roles in the killing remain to be established.

**Last development:** Earlier Somnolent investigations; Prusset's death remains relevant to the active Eden and Summer thread.

---

### Break into Alaaric's safe

**Urgency:** Closed â€” failed  
**Theme:** Bastion / Alaaric Hammerfall

**Objective:** Open [[03 People/Notable Figures/Alaaric Hammerfall|Alaaric Hammerfall's]] secured safe.

**Current position:** **Failed / abandoned.** The safe branded n'Dong and Erelmor and remained shut.

**Key history:** The failed attempt is recorded as a concluded plan, not an open task; nothing in the current tracker indicates an active renewed attempt.

**Last development:** Earlier campaign; exact session not specified in the original tracker.

---

### Recover the relic from Rana Viam

**Urgency:** Closed  
**Theme:** Ardynia / Rana Viam

**Objective:** Defeat the ruin's guardian and secure its principal relic.

**Current position:** **Completed.** The Defracturers destroyed the golem and recovered the Bands of Distortion and their heart stone.

**Key history:** The retrieval was completed at Rana Viam. **Attunement, the scroll's contents and decisions about the Bands' ultimate custody remain unresolved** under the broader Sundering relic thread.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 19 - Bands, Broken Stone and the Glass Fang|Session 19]] (recovery).

---

### Identify who ordered Anthea's assassination

**Urgency:** Closed  
**Theme:** Anthea Bloodclot / Perfid / Rootbound

**Objective:** Establish who commissioned the attack on Anthea Bloodclot.

**Current position:** **Completed at the conspiracy level.** The evidence points to the Church of Elgira and Perfidian interests acting through Prusset, who employed a Rootbound cell.

**Key history:** The order and intermediaries have been traced, but **the identity of the direct killer remains unresolved** in a separate inactive thread. In Session 25, Elmwood additionally identified Anthea as Saint Marra from the Sundering memory.

**Last development:** [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 25 - Ashes, Arrogance, and the Blood of Ages|Session 25]] (new information about Anthea's identity); the assassination commission was established earlier.

# Anthea Bloodclotâ€™s Note on Tace Nox

![[Anthea Bloodclot's Note on Tace Nox.png|500]]

## Transcription

> To whatever poor fool reads this â€” allow me to save you some teeth-grinding.
>
> The lights must form an arrow. Thatâ€™s the key. Point it at the altar, or youâ€™ll be swarmed. Youâ€™ve got six moves. No more. Six.
>
> Why an arrow? Because of course the Ardynians â€” curse be their brittle, egotistical bones â€” could never pass up a chance to overcomplicate a situation. Gods forbid they just wrote: â€œPush here to not die.â€
>
> I swear, every vault, every lock, every tomb of theirs is a shrine to smug superiority. Ageless? Yes. But if you outlive death and still need to solve three riddles and cast a starlight incantation to open your bloody wine cabinet, you havenâ€™t conquered time â€” youâ€™ve wasted it.
>
> Iâ€™ve already lost three crows to the damned thing, only Eden and I remain. If I ever find a chronarch that survived, if just one managed to evade Elgiraâ€™s clutches, I will put a knife through their eye and scatter their ashes across Rakara.
>
> A.B.

The source image also contains a flower-like mark and several columns of symbols. These have been preserved in the image but not interpreted.

## Significance

- Gives the solution to the six-move light puzzle in Tace Nox.
- Indicates that Anthea had lost three Crows and that only she and Eden remained in the context described by the note. It does not establish whether other Crows existed elsewhere or when Summer Quaresh acquired the role.
- Shows Antheaâ€™s hostility towards surviving Chronarchs and suggests Elgira acted against them.

# Broken Fang Camp Sketches

> [!summary] At a glance
> Two crude maps drawn by the captured Broken Fang scouts Salla and Malt, showing the camp commanded by [[03 People/Notable Deceased/Ulsar Quinn|Ulsar Quinn]] and the approximate location of the Defracturers' stolen horses.

## Circumstances

During the pursuit outside [[02 Places/Rakara/Rana Viam|Rana Viam]], the Defracturers captured two fleeing Broken Fang scouts: **Salla** and **Malt**.

The party demanded that each scout draw a map of the nearby camp, placing particular emphasis on the location where the stolen horses were being held. The request was presented as a contest in which useful information might determine which prisoner survived.

Both scouts produced a sketch.

## Salla's sketch

<!-- Add the corresponding image to _Assets and embed it here.
Suggested filename: Broken Fang Camp Sketch - Salla.png
Suggested embed: ![[Broken Fang Camp Sketch - Salla.png|600]]
-->

Salla's map was crude and sparse. She died later in the session during an attack by Egg Hunter Hatchlings, preventing the party from questioning her further.

## Malt's sketch

<!-- Add the corresponding image to _Assets and embed it here.
Suggested filename: Broken Fang Camp Sketch - Malt.png
Suggested embed: ![[Broken Fang Camp Sketch - Malt.png|600]]
-->

Malt's map was similarly rough. Malt survived the hatchling attack after Ieuan stabilised him and remained in the Defracturers' custody at the end of Session 20.

## Comparison and reliability

The two sketches were similar enough that the Defracturers considered their broad layout believable.

Information that can be treated with the greatest confidence:

- a Broken Fang camp exists nearby;
- it is associated with Ulsar Quinn;
- the Defracturers' horses are being held there;
- both prisoners described broadly the same arrangement.

Information that remains unreliable or incomplete:

- precise scale and distance;
- compass orientation;
- the number and positions of guards;
- patrol routes;
- fortifications, traps and hidden approaches;
- whether the camp layout changed after the scouts departed.

The sketches should be treated as reconnaissance aids rather than accurate surveyed maps.

## Current significance

The maps are the party's principal source of information for planning an infiltration or assault to recover:

- Kingcup;
- Bracken;
- Nail;
- Vesper;
- Cobb.

It is not yet known whether the camp has been warned about the missing scouts or the party's possession of the [[Bands of Distortion|Bands of Distortion]].

> [!question] Unresolved
> - Which route offers the safest approach?
> - How many Broken Fang members occupy the camp?
> - Where is Ulsar Quinn within the camp?
> - Are the horses guarded, restrained or prepared for movement?
> - Has the camp learned that Salla and Malt were captured?
> - Did either scout deliberately omit important defences?

# Broken Fang Contraband Manifest

> [!summary] At a glance
> A manifest recovered by n'Dong from a concealed Broken Fang drop site, identifying a supply network connecting locations across Rakara.

## Recovery

n'Dong discovered the manifest inside a shack used to store smuggled supplies near Somnolent's docks.

The site contained crates, whiskey and wrapped bricks of an unidentified substance. After sampling one of the bricks, n'Dong suffered severe pain, convulsions and temporary incapacity. The party later identified the substance on the manifest as **Agony**.

## Reconstructed transcription

The following entries are preserved in the Session 7 record:

| Destination | Recorded delivery |
|---|---|
| **One Ball Inn, Somnolent** | 4 x barrels of whiskey |
| **The Scythian Tree, Orvak** | 2 x rations; 4 x weapons or armour |
| **Fighting Pits, Voruun** | Agony; Powdered Power; Dragon's Dandruff |
| **Ashka's Den, Khaali** | Unicorn Wax; Ember Berries |

## Significance

The manifest shows that the [[04 Organisations/Broken Fang|Broken Fang]] operated a distribution network extending across several Rakaran territories.

It also established that:

- the [[02 Places/Rakara/Somnolent/One Ball Inn|One Ball Inn]] was part of the supply route;
- weapons and armour were being moved towards Orvak;
- unusual substances were being delivered to the Voruun fighting pits;
- a separate Khaali contact or destination received rare materials;
- the Somnolent shipment was only one part of a wider operation.

The party later learned from [[03 People/Notable Deceased/Ulsar Quinn|Ulsar Quinn]] that [[06 Library/Campaign Reference/Dragon's Dandruff|Dragon's Dandruff]] is a potent stimulant used by contestants in the fighting pits of [[02 Places/Rakara/Voruun/Dazuun|Dazuun]]. Three Khaali alchemists are producing it for [[03 People/Notable Figures/Rhul Slowdrift|Rhul Slowdrift's]] operation from a lab south of [[02 Places/Rakara/Somnolent/Somnolent|Somnolent]].

The purpose and legality of each listed substance have not all been confirmed.

> [!question] Unresolved
> - Who prepared the manifest?
> - Who collected each delivery?
> - What are Powdered Power, Unicorn Wax and Ember Berries?
> - Why were weapons and armour being sent to the Scythian Tree?
> - Is Ashka a person, organisation or place?
> - How much of the wider distribution network survives after the destruction of the One Ball Inn?

## Campaign reference

- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 07 - Coffee and Contraband|Session 7]]
- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 21 - Contracts, Command, and a Burning Camp|Session 21]]
- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 24 - Pies, Pike and a one ball blaze|Session 24]]

## Session 24 confirmation

The One Ball connection did not remain merely historical. During the Defracturers' absence, Bastion staff overheard men pressuring [[03 People/Notable Figures/Antoinne Cassan|Antoinne Cassan]] about the inn's use as an easy place to **fence** goods. The men explicitly referred to [[03 People/Notable Figures/Rhul Slowdrift|Rhul Slowdrift]] and the need for discretion.

A separate barrel abandoned by fleeing smugglers contained expensive goods with merchant markings stripped or obscured and was later connected to Antoinne. Shortly afterwards Antoinne disappeared and the One Ball Inn burned down.

The open question is therefore no longer whether the One Ball network was still active, but how much of the wider route survives after the loss of that node.

# Instrument of Patronage & Service

> [!info] Signed source and interpretation
> This page is a **reference summary** of the signed six-page *Instrument of Patronage & Service*, issued by Headman [[03 People/Notable Figures/Romayne Drayton|Romayne Drayton]] for the Defracturers. **The actual signed contract controls if this summary differs.** 

## Parties, purpose and duration (Sections 1â€“2)

- **Patron:** Romayne Drayton, in his official capacity as Headman of Somnolent. **Agents:** the undersigned adventuring company, collectively.
- The company acts as a discreet instrument of Somnolent's interests inside and beyond the town, in good faith and with reasonable competence.
- The arrangement continues until lawfully terminated. It does **not** automatically expire after an initial mission.

## Bastion, staff and upkeep (Section 3)

- The Agents receive **exclusive residence and use** of the former Perfid embassy for the contract's duration, subject to its terms. Permitted uses include housing, headquarters, storage, planning, defensive redoubt, meetings and staging sanctioned operations.
- The Patron provides a **basic serving staff** for cleaning, cooking, pantry, simple workshops, library and non-denominational chapel maintenance. The Patron may requisition staff for Somnolent's safety.
- The Patron pays ordinary structural maintenance, routine repairs, fuel, linen and basic food and drink **consumed on the premises**.
- **Excluded without separate arrangements:** luxury food and drink, rare/exotic goods, reagents and alchemical stocks, war-grade fortifications, bespoke work, redesign, new facilities, additional staff, and new Silent Court permits/applications.
- These provisions do not grant unlimited control over public staff or an obligation for Somnolent to fund arbitrary renovations.

## Diplomatic standing (Section 4)

- The Agents receive immunity **equivalent to ambassadors within Somnolent's jurisdiction**, only as far as the Headman's lawful authority reaches. It protects against routine arrest/detention and normally treats the Bastion as a protected diplomatic residence.
- The Agents may request access to civil authorities through the Patron's office.
- Town officers may enter in a **dire emergency** (fire, plague, rampaging creature or immediate danger) and must notify the Patron and Agents as soon as practical.
- The immunity **does not excuse wanton harm** to Somnolent or its citizens, or protect acts formally declared Rogue Acts. This is **not** universal immunity from foreign authorities or all consequences.

## Assignments, readiness and refusal (Sections 5â€“6, 8)

- Sanctioned tasks may include exploration, intelligence, infiltration, protection, force, external diplomacy and recovery/custody of people, documents or dangerous items.
- The company is expected to remain reasonably ready to depart urgently, **subject to the right of refusal**.
- Agents may refuse work that is plainly suicidal, requires unjustified betrayal of an ally, risks disproportionate war/catastrophe, or violates a sincerely held oath disclosed in good faith. They must explain and, where possible, offer an alternative or request reasonable resources.
- Repeated unjustified refusal can result in reduced stipend/support or termination.

## Stipend, expenses and recovered property (Section 7)

- **Monthly stipend:** the signed manuscript appears to specify **1,000 gold per month for the company**, payable on the first day of each month and distributed internally by the Agents. 
- For sanctioned assignments, reasonable upfront mundane travel, lodging, bribes, consumables and healing supplies can be provided. Additional reimbursements are discretionary on receipt or sworn accounting.
- **Coins and common items** acquired on commission belong entirely to the party (the first printed clause numbered 7.3).
- **Uncommon, wondrous or rare items** recovered on commission must be **truthfully declared to the Patron within 24 hours of return to Somnolent**. They must not be sold or consumed before declaration unless immediately necessary for safety. Concealment, undeclared sale/transfer or deliberate misdescription is a material breach and may be treated as a Rogue Act (the second printed clause numbered 7.3).
- After declaration, the Patron may allow use of an item, subject to later revocation. Sensitive or dangerous property goes into **Silent Court custody**. The signed contract does **not** automatically designate Aalaric's private safe as the custody location.
- **Saleable property not needed by the Agents or Somnolent:** after declaration, either exchange it with the Patron for **60% of fair face value**, or sell legally and keep **70% of the actual proceeds or half of fair face value, whichever is higher**. The remainder of the sale proceeds must be paid to the Patron within **seven days**, with accounting. This is the contract's precise formula, not a flat '40%' or '60%' rule for every item.
- **Unknown/magic/possibly cursed or sentient items (Section 7.4):** they go for approved study by Elmwood, the Company, or another approved peer, normally in interim **Silent Court custody**. After identification, the Patron decides case by case whether to permit company use, secure storage, named-mission use or removal from circulation. There is **no unconditional right to keep every artefact**.

## Conduct and Rogue Acts (Section 10)

- Agents must not **abuse staff, civilians or prisoners**, incite riots for personal gain, betray sensitive Somnolent information, or admit hostile armed groups to the Bastion. Confidentiality is expected where reasonable.
- A Rogue Act is an act materially endangering Somnolent or directly defying a written prohibition from the Patron. Following its **formal declaration**, immunity may be suspended and the agreement terminated for cause.

## Ending the contract and retrospective pardon (Sections 9, 11â€“12)

- **Three-day grace:** during the first three full days after execution, any Agent may walk away without penalty. Remaining Agents can continue as a smaller company or dissolve the agreement. **Using this provision voids the retrospective pardon for the withdrawing Agent.**
- Afterwards, the Agents may end the contract with **seven days' notice**, except urgent situations where prompt subsequent notice is accepted. The Patron may terminate immediately for a declared Rogue Act, murder of a citizen without cause, or material collaboration with Somnolent's enemies.
- On termination, the Agents must vacate within **seven days**, return official keys, seals and papers, and leave staff unharmed; personal belongings may be removed.
- Section 11.5 states that stipend owed on a pro-rata basis is repayable and that post-termination funding becomes a **short-term loan charging 0.8% interest per day**. The exact calculation/application is **not further explained** in the signed text; avoid inventing a repayment formula.
- The Patron gives a pardon for eligible **Somnolent-jurisdiction offences in the 24 hours immediately preceding signature**. Exceptions include citizen murder, treason, catastrophic sacrilege and already adjudicated crimes. Foreign jurisdictions and private debts/grievances are not bound.

## Disputes (Section 13)
The Parties must first seek private mediation from the Patron; unresolved disputes may go to Somnolent's designated magistrate, discreetly where possible.

## Campaign links

- [[02 Places/Rakara/Somnolent/The Defracturers' Bastion|The Defracturers' Bastion]]
- [[04 Organisations/The Defracturers|The Defracturers]]
- [[03 People/Notable Figures/Romayne Drayton|Romayne Drayton]]
- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 25 - Ashes, Arrogance, and the Blood of Ages|Session 25]]
- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 26 - Sundering Relics and the Market Brawl|Session 26]]

## Application in Session 27

Alaaric cited the existing provision allowing staff to be requisitioned for duties relating to Somnolentâ€™s safety during Romayneâ€™s dispute with the party. Romayne also requested review and possible safeguarding of dangerous relics in Alaaricâ€™s safe, with arrangements for access. This records the application of the existing instrument, not a contractual amendment or proof of a handover. No formal Rogue Act declaration or termination was recorded.

See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 27 - Gayles Warning and the Dandruff Lab|Session 27]].

# Report on the Death of Anthea Bloodclot

| | |
|---|---|
| **Classification** | Murder |
| **Status at filing** | Unsolved |
| **Death discovered** | 21st Greengold, 5137 |
| **Report entered** | 29th Greengold, 5137 |
| **Keeper of record** | [[Alaaric Hammerfall]] |

[Open the original report](../../_Assets/Documents/Report%20on%20the%20Death%20of%20Anthea%20Bloodclot.docx)

## Record

On the morning of the [[Emberwake Festival]], Anthea Bloodclot was discovered dead inside her caravan, stationed approximately two miles east of [[Somnolent]] along the southern road. She had been scheduled to address the gathered people later that day. Her attendance had been promoted most strongly by [[Alistair Prusset]], envoy of Perfid, and [[Eden Carrion]], liaison of Clan Voruun.

The body was discovered by **Sable Greentree**, a local merchant employed by the town to deliver festival supplies.

## Known recent contacts

- **[[Callum Redmayne]]** saw Anthea on the evening before her death with Eden Carrion and **[[Summer Quaresh]]**, sharing drinks.
- **[[Elmwood Bluevine]]** reported that Anthea had visited him one week earlier seeking information about a venture into Thistlewound.
- **[[Ersil Loquella]]** spoke with her two days before her death while gathering material for a ballad.

## Cause of death

Initial examination suggested natural causes. **[[Father Zantos]]**, Elder Druid of Somnolent, identified signs consistent with poisoning by **Bloodspore**, a rare and potent herb cultivated exclusively in Clan Orvak's lands.

The poison was probably administered in the wine found inside the caravan, which remained half-consumed.

## Loss of the crime scene

No comprehensive inquiry was conducted by the town guard. During the Emberwake festivities, the caravan and all evidence within it were removed by unknown parties. This prevented preservation of the scene and any later complete examination.

## Suspected interests

The report records several possible motives without assigning guilt:

- The **Rootbound** possessed access to Bloodspore and had openly opposed Anthea, but may have lacked the wealth and logistics to act alone.
- **Somnolent** resisted incorporation into Anthea's project, and [[Romayne Drayton]] had publicly opposed attempts to force the town into the fold.
- **Doro** feared the trade and military consequences of a unified Rakara.
- **Perfid** had condemned Anthea's interest in Ardynian history as heretical, despite Prusset publicly championing her Emberwake appearance.
- **Minir** was rumoured to have supported some of Anthea's early activity before becoming quieter as her power grew.

## Later significance

The report established Bloodspore poisoning and the removal of the caravan as central facts in the party's investigation. Later evidence connected Perfid, Alistair Prusset and a Rootbound cell to the murder; those conclusions were not available when this document was entered into Somnolent's records.

# Stolen Letter from Alistair Prussetâ€™s Study

![[Letter from Arch-Illuminant Serathiel.png|500]]

## Transcription

> Ambassador Prussett,
>
> The High Council has reviewed your concerns regarding the Bloodclot woman. Her defiance is not merely political â€” it is theological trespass. Her elevation threatens the balance we have laboured to maintain.
>
> We concur that decisive action is now justified. You are advised to seek the rootbound. They operate beneath the canopy, but their reach extends far beyond it. They understand silence, and how to gift it.
>
> As ever, discretion is paramount.
>
> Arch-Illuminant Serathiel.

## Significance

The letter directly authorises decisive action against Anthea Bloodclot and instructs Prusset to contact the Rootbound.

> [!info] Church offices
> **Arch-Illuminant Serathiel** and **High Illuminant Derathel** are different people in different offices. Derathel is the head of the Church; Serathiel is the senior official who signed this instruction.

# FILE: C:\Users\ellis\Documents\vaelond-wiki\content\06 Library\Letters and Documents\Vaelen's memory stone.md
---

---
title: Vaelen's memory stone
type: document
status: published
player_facing: true
aliases:
  - Vaelen's Sending Stone
  - Vaelen sending stone
tags:
  - library
  - document
  - ardynia
  - chronarchs
  - campaign
source: Session 17
updated: 2026-10-03
---

My end is near. I am certain, even if I cannot yet name the hand that will finally reach me. If I am fortunate, this record will outlive me. If I am not, then perhaps it will merely serve as proof that at least one of us understood the charge laid upon us.

We were not meant to rule. That is the truth the others have forgotten. We were charged to guide the world, to prepare it, to leave behind something strong enough to resist should the ritual ever fail and Ardynia rise again. We were to cultivate endurance, not dominion. Readiness, not empire. We were never meant to lead the world by the throat.

Of them all, Erith has held closest to that truth. He built what he wished to build, gave Minir its structure, and then stepped away. Some would call that selfishness. Perhaps it is. He has spent his long years pursuing pleasure and his own amusements while the rest of us tangled ourselves in duty and fear. Yet there is honesty in him. He understood that this world was meant to stand on its own feet, not remain beneath ours. I do not admire his indifference, but I understand it better than the ambitions of the others.

Marra understands none of this. She believes our charge unfinished. Worse, she believes herself chosen to complete it. She wants the relics not to preserve them, but to wield them â€” to finish what Elgira began and destroy the threat of Ardynia utterly, whatever the cost. She speaks of necessity, as zealots always do when they wish to make horror sound disciplined. To her, my refusal to join her is proof that I stand against her. She cannot imagine restraint except as betrayal. I believe she comes for me, and I believe she will call it righteousness when she does.

Desmon is no better, only more vain. He has grown tired of immortality in mortal shape, tired of being served by the fettered masses of Perfid. The long years have not humbled him; they have only made him greedy for a grander form of survival. He looks upon Elgiraâ€™s ascension and sees not accident, not tragedy, but possibility. He wants to rise as she did â€” no, more than she did, because he would choose it. He has begun to think like a man who sees divinity as an attainment. I do not trust what he would become if given the means.

Perhaps Erith is the wiser of us. Willingly giving up power was the only honest act any of us committed after Elgira ascended. Yet even now I cannot follow him fully. He can watch the pieces fall. I still feel compelled to place my hand between the board and those who would overturn it. For that, I think I shall die.

I confess I do not meet that prospect of death with grace. I fear it. I fear ending. So I have made preparations. If my enemies take my body, they shall not so easily claim all of me. I have preserved what I can preserve. My soul, at least, will not be surrendered willingly to oblivion. If this is cowardice, then it is cowardice I embrace gladly.

â€” Vaelen

# Eula's Red Coins

> [!summary] At a glance
> Reddish gold coins marked by tracking magic and used by [[03 People/Notable Figures/Eula|Eula]] when purchasing corpses. Eula is alerted when someone is killed in the presence of one of the coins.

## Appearance

The coins resemble ordinary gold at a glance but possess a distinctive reddish hue. Magical examination has revealed arcane markers associated with tracking.

The party first encountered the marked currency after a hooded woman paid Erelmor for the bodies of slain bandits. The same woman later identified herself as Eula.

## Known behaviour

The Defracturers have established or observed that:

- Eula pays marked coin in exchange for fresh corpses.
- The coins alert Eula when someone is killed in the presence of one of the coins.
- Eula may appear rapidly through teleportation magic to collect bodies and conduct a transaction.
- Placing a coin on a body that was already dead did not immediately summon her.
- The coins remain usable as currency, but carrying or spending them may spread Eula's influence or allow her to track events connected to them.

The exact range and limitations of the magic remain unknown.

## Campaign record

### First encounter

After a roadside battle, an unidentified hooded woman appeared and offered sixty gold for the defeated bandits. The party discovered arcane tracking marks on the payment and discarded the coins.

### Eula identifies herself

The party later found fifty reddish coins on a bandit captain. Eula appeared and doubled their value in exchange for the bodies of the dead bandits.

Erelmor attempted to give one of the coins to a farmer, but Ieuan replaced it with ordinary gold before the exchange was completed.

Erelmor also placed one of the coins on an already dead body as an experiment. Eula did not immediately appear.

### The ogres

After the Defracturers killed three ogres, Eula arrived and paid seventy-five marked coins for the bodies. Questioning her established that the coins notify her when someone is killed in the presence of one of them.

## Session 22 and current status

At Ulsar Quinn's destroyed camp, Erelmor attempted to return **every piece of tainted gold he had accumulated** by slipping the coins into Eula's pocket. The party then refused further participation in Eula's corpse trade.

The record does not explicitly inventory every marked coin held by every party member after this exchange, so the current holder and exact number remaining should be treated as **uncertain** rather than assuming the Defracturers still possess them.

Eula also gave the clearest statement yet of her wider motive: **"Ardynia is returning, and I intend to be prepared, with or without your help."** This narrows her purpose but does not explain what she does with the bodies or how they relate to those preparations.

> [!warning] Caution
> The coins should not be treated as ordinary treasure. Their tracking effect may expose the holder, nearby deaths or the party's movements to Eula if violence occurs in their presence.

> [!question] Unresolved
> - How close must a killing be to a coin for Eula to be alerted?
> - Does spending a coin transfer the mark to a new holder?
> - Can the magic be removed?
> - How does Eula intend to use the bodies to prepare for Ardynia's return?
> - Who or what receives the corpses after she takes them?

# False Gardener's Notebook

> A magically infused notebook recovered from the ruined home of [[03 People/Mentioned Figures/Maelor Vey|Maelor Vey, the False Gardener]], capable of allowing its holder to cast **Speak with Plants** with remarkable regularity.

## Recovery

Ballar found the notebook after the Defracturers destroyed the immediate corruption beneath Maelor's home in Session 22. He immediately used it to speak with [[Bushtache|Bushtache]].

Before leaving the forest in Session 23, Ballar handed the notebook to [[03 People/Player Characters/Ieuan|Ieuan]].

## Known contents

Ieuan briefly consulted Maelor's notes about the nearby bog siren. The notebook indicated that the creature had been made through the same corrupted magic as the black ooze and [[Plague|Plague]], and that it did not merely resist acid but appeared to thrive upon it.

The full contents and limits of the notebook remain unknown.

# Star Seed

> [!summary] At a glance
> A mysterious devotional seed gifted to [[03 People/Player Characters/Ieuan|Ieuan]] by [[03 People/Notable Figures/Father Zantos|Father Zantos]]. Its power is awakening gradually as Ieuan deepens his understanding of the forces represented within it.

## Origin

Father Zantos gave the Star Seed to Ieuan during their conversation in Somnolent and promised that it would bloom once Ieuan fully embraced his druidic path.

Their discussion connected Ieuan's Circle of Stars path with Orun, ancient truths preserved by Clan Erzhin and Zantos's own difficult history with the Rootbound.

## Development

In Session 21, Ieuan studied the Star Seed during the rest before the attack on Ulsar's camp. His concentration was repeatedly interrupted by [[Plague|Plague]].

During Session 23 he tried again. Approaching the seed through **nature** failed when Plague's voice overwhelmed his thoughts with the command *Spread*.

Later, Ieuan approached the Seed through **faith**, reaching towards [[01 Welcome to Vaelond/Religion/Rakaran Faiths#Orun, the Verdant Kin|Orun]], the Moon-Father and the Sea-Mother and recalling the teachings of Father Zantos and the elders of his youth.

The Seed grew warm. Silver light traced across it and another leaf unfurled, shifting between sage green, moonlit silver and the blue-white foam of the sea.

## Known properties

Ieuan now understands that:

- the Star Seed is a **devotional object**, not merely a passive charm;
- its power can be awakened gradually through patient understanding of the forces it represents;
- **Star Forms** lie dormant within it;
- at least one further stage of growth has been triggered through faith;
- **nature** remains an unresolved part of the Seed's meaning or awakening.

Its complete abilities and final form remain unknown.

> [!question] Unresolved
> - What must Ieuan understand about nature to continue awakening the Seed?
> - What are the dormant Star Forms and how will they manifest?
> - What will the Seed become when fully awakened?
> - Did Zantos create, inherit or recover it?
> - How directly is it tied to Orun, the Sea-Mother, the Moon-Father or the Circle of Stars?
> - Why is Plague able to obstruct Ieuan's attempts to approach it through nature?

## Session 26 â€” meditation with Zantos

In Somnolent, Ieuan prayed with [[03 People/Notable Figures/Father Zantos|Father Zantos]] and Mother Joygrove, then spoke to Zantos about the tension between his growing connection to the stars and his earlier closeness to the earth. With Zantosâ€™s help he meditated on the remaining **natural** aspect of the Star Seed but **did not complete the breakthrough**. Zantos counselled patience and reconnection with his roots over time. See [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 26 - Sundering Relics and the Market Brawl|Session 26]].

# Verei's Shattered Mask

> [!summary] At a glance
> An old brittle mask found beneath the Drowsy Coin, which forced the Defracturers into private visions before n'Dong invoked Verei, the Shattered Mask, and lifted its curse. The mask now appears to act almost like an arcane focus for receiving Verei's blessing, though that blessing may be dangerous.

## Discovery

The mask was found in the basement of the [[02 Places/Rakara/Somnolent/Drowsy Coin|Drowsy Coin]] after Callum Redmayne asked the Defracturers to investigate something strange below the tavern.

Ballar kicked through a pile of crates and struck the mask, causing it to slide free from the wreckage.

## The visions

When the mask was disturbed, the Defracturers were pulled into isolating visions or memories.

Known visions included:

- n'Dong seeing [[03 People/Notable Figures/Prodosius|Prodosius]] boast of slaughtering his parents and warning that n'Dong would soon die;
- Ieuan seeing a wood elf poisoner whispering of betrayal;
- Enrak standing before Ashka, goddess of fire and chaos;
- Ballar being rejected by the image of his mother;
- Erelmor seeing his dying wife begging him to explain why he had not saved her.

When the visions fractured, apparitions of Ballar's mother and Erelmor's wife remained and attacked the party.

## The prayer to Verei

After the apparitions were defeated, n'Dong approached the mask and recited a forgotten prayer to **Verei, the Shattered Mask**, whose broken visage watches over secrets and spirits.

The curse lifted. The mask fell inert, and n'Dong took it.

## Known properties

The mask has demonstrated the ability to:

- trigger intensely personal visions or memories;
- manifest hostile spiritual apparitions;
- draw upon guilt, grief, fear or hidden trauma;
- respond to a prayer associated with Verei.

After n'Dong's prayer, the mask appeared inert, but it should not be treated as powerless. It appears to function almost like an arcane focus, making it easier for the bearer to receive a blessing from Verei. Such a blessing is not necessarily safe, beneficial or under the bearer's control.

## Current status

n'Dong currently carries the mask. It is best understood as dormant rather than harmless: an object that can focus Verei's influence and make blessings easier to receive, while leaving the cost, danger and control of those blessings unresolved.

> [!warning] Caution
> The mask should not be assumed safe merely because the immediate curse was lifted. Its connection to secrets, spirits, personal trauma and Verei's blessings remains unexplained.

> [!question] Unresolved
> - What is Verei?
> - Why did n'Dong know the prayer?
> - Was the mask a relic, holy object, cursed object or prison?
> - Why was it hidden beneath the Drowsy Coin?
> - Can the mask still reveal secrets or summon spirits?
> - Did the visions show literal truth, manipulated memory or symbolic fear?

## Campaign reference

- [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 07 - Coffee and Contraband|Session 7]]

## Session 25 â€” crypt shrine

n'Dong established a shrine to Verei in the crypt of the Defracturers' Bastion and received a powerful blessing. The chronicle does not specify the blessing's mechanical effects, duration or cost, and does not establish that the mask itself changed. See [[03 People/Player Characters/n'Dong|n'Dong]] and [[05 Campaign Chronicle/Session 25 - Ashes, Arrogance, and the Blood of Ages|Session 25]].
